## What is corrected

Original Fudan `line_z` rewards positive root vz while in flight and has no height-shortfall factor.130 added that factor to its conservative actual-wheel-ascent adaptation. At own extension geometry, root/both wheel-centre/bottom vz=.6, both wheel bottoms5–64mm, all other quantities fixed, the TOTAL130 height derivative is negative (about-.01512reward/m at10mm). The new term removes exactly the negative `-.025/.27` contribution. Full derivation and source lines are in the frozen review; the new fixture measures TOTAL derivatives using actual own FK rigid-height translations and checks the analytical whole-interval sign.

New selected formula is `.01*effort*clamp(6*conservative_up,0,2.5)*air*(1-P)*region*active`. No weight/cap, forward command, own leg target, readiness/flight/cross logic, current air surrogate, effort/closure term, original success counter or physical input changes. All13 selected term names and API shapes are identical to130. The required runtime remains130 over127 with this single additional file override;128 is not a dependency.

`SOURCE_CONTRACT133.json` declares exact formula, all selected names and conservative term/total bounds. Total request bound is still[0,.19], inherited conservative executor cap.20. Before effort/closure: tracker+yaw<=.03, jump<=.0771*(1-P), top+quiet<=.16P, so subtotal<=.19. Closure removes at most half. Nonrequest recovery remains the original potentially signed branch.

def own_wheel_height_score(bottom2,step_height=.27):
    """Fudan exp(-6*abs(height-target)), in actual wheel-bottom coordinates.

    Remove its floor offset and normalize BEFORE scaling to the original
    per-term2.5 cap. Thus zero at floor and strictly increasing to270mm;
    the original6*exp then clip would saturate well BEFORE its target.
    """
    floor=bottom2.new_tensor(-6*step_height).exp()
    each=((torch.exp(-6*(bottom2-step_height).abs())-floor)/(1-floor)).clamp(0,1)
    return each.amin(-1)


def evaluate(m,mode,legacy_nonrequest,effort_quality,*,launched,crossed,closure_m,public_age,p=CommandReward()):
    P=mode['top_support'];v=m['world_velocity3'];active=m['request_active']
    landing=P*crossed.to(P.dtype)
    desired_x=mode['base_command_vx']*(1-landing)
    error=(v[...,0]-desired_x).square()+v[...,1].square()
    # Original tracking and enhanced kernels/weights. Limit the enhanced cost
    # to its matching positive tracker: a bad command cannot make total reward
    # negative and reward early death. This is a declared safety adaptation.
    tracking=2*torch.exp(-error/.25)
    enhanced=torch.maximum(2*(torch.exp(-error/2.5)-1),-tracking)
    heading=(1-m['heading_cos'].clamp(-1,1)).clamp_min(0)
    alignment=torch.exp(-heading/(1-.9800665778412416)-m['root_y'].square()/.3**2)
    leading_front=m['wheel_high2'][...,0].amax(-1)
    before_edge=smooth01((p.edge_x-leading_front)/.04)
    bottom=m['wheel_bottom2'];minimum=bottom.amin(-1)
    H=own_wheel_height_score(bottom,p.step_height)
    clearance=(minimum/p.step_height).clamp(0,1)
    # Current observable geometry + two-control wheel-force history surrogate.
    # NO ready prerequisite, no16-callback prerequisite, no vx/near prerequisite.
    # This permits dense takeoff learning in the actual observed ready=0 branch.
    # It never changes the stricter original physical/process qualification.
    air=smooth01(minimum/.005)*smooth01(m['nonwheel_clearance_m']/.005)*m['unloaded_both']
    stage=torch.maximum(air,launched.to(v.dtype))
    # Preserve height/tuck credit through a qualified partial-footprint landing.
    # Otherwise first real top contact would erase the air terms before P rises.
    region=m['task_lane_and_far_bound'].to(v.dtype)
    jump=(1-P)*region
    upward=v[...,2].clamp(0,2.4)  # original.15*vz+, bounded at inherited engineering reference
    own_lift=torch.minimum(m['wheel_bottom_velocity2'],m['wheel_centre_velocity2']).amin(-1)
    conservative_up=torch.minimum(v[...,2],own_lift).clamp_min(0)
    line_z=(6*conservative_up).clamp_max(2.5)*air
    length=m['virtual_leg_length2']
    extend=torch.exp(-4*(length-m['own_extension_length2']).abs().sum(-1))
    tuck=torch.exp(-4*(length-m['own_tuck_length2']).abs().sum(-1))
    # Preserve Fudan's support + root-vz>.15 takeoff definition, with measured
    # support history as an explicitly imperfect proxy. No mandatory crouch.
    takeoff=m['supported_any']*(v[...,2]>.15).to(v.dtype)*before_edge
    quiet=torch.exp(-v.square().sum(-1)/.03**2-m['world_angular_velocity3'].square().sum(-1)/.3**2
        -(1-m['upright_cos'].clamp(-1,1)).clamp_min(0)/(1-.984807753012208)
        -heading/(1-.9800665778412416))
    dt=.01*effort_quality
    quality=(closure_m/.002).clamp(0,1).square()*(public_age>=10).to(v.dtype)
    terms=dict(fudan_tracking_lin_vel=dt*tracking*alignment*active,
        fudan_tracking_lin_vel_enhance=dt*enhanced*alignment*active,
        fudan_tracking_ang_vel=dt*torch.exp(-m['body_yaw_velocity'].square()/.25)*active,
        fudan_encourage_jump_upward=dt*.15*upward*jump*active,
        fudan_takeoff_extend_own_geometry=dt*.5*extend*takeoff*jump*active,
        fudan_line_z_real_wheel_ascent=dt*line_z*jump*active,
        fudan_flight_real_clearance=dt*.15*stage*clearance*jump*active,
        fudan_base_height_flight_own_wheel270=dt*2.5*H*stage*jump*active,
        fudan_leg_tuck_own_geometry=dt*1.7*tuck*stage*clearance*jump*active,
        supported_top_after_qualified_cross=dt*12*landing*active,
        quiet_top_after_qualified_cross=dt*4*landing*quiet*active,
        closure_compliance_cost=torch.zeros_like(dt),
        original_nonrequest_recovery=(1-active)*legacy_nonrequest)
    positive=sum(value for name,value in terms.items() if name not in ('closure_compliance_cost','original_nonrequest_recovery'))
    terms['closure_compliance_cost']=-.5*quality*positive
    total=sum(terms.values())
    if not bool(torch.isfinite(total).all()):raise ValueError('nonfinite130 reward')
    return dict(total=total,selected_terms=terms,command_vx=desired_x,
        command_wheel_bottom_height=.27,command_wheel_bottom_upward_velocity=torch.full_like(H,2.4),
        planning_top_support=P,qualified_cross=crossed,before_edge_gate=before_edge,
        both_wheel_height_score=H,closure_quality_cost=quality,
        dense_air_geometry_history_surrogate=air,dense_stage_with_qualified_landing_bridge=stage,
        true_wheel_clearance_fraction=clearance,conservative_upward_velocity=conservative_up,
        own_extension_length2=m['own_extension_length2'],own_tuck_length2=m['own_tuck_length2'],
        process_ready=m['process_ready'],dense_incentives_do_not_require_ready=True,
        process126_unchanged=True,upstream_commit=UPSTREAM_COMMIT,
        original_stateful_airtime_accumulator_not_selected=True,
        source_only_not_runtime_admitted=True,not_original_Fudan_two_policy_reward=True)


def combine_closure_risk(old_soft_done,closure_m,public_age):
    """Continuous engineering constraint-risk cost, not calibrated failure probability.

    The physical2mm guard is unchanged. Predicted1.2..1.8mm now has nonzero
    cost even though the WM underestimates the saved actual failure. This does
    not claim the underlying WM closure prediction has been repaired.
    """
    risk=(closure_m/.002).clamp(0,1).square()
    risk=torch.where(public_age>=10,risk,torch.zeros_like(risk))
    per_step=-torch.expm1(torch.log1p(-risk.clamp_max(1-1e-6))/32.)
    combined=1-(1-old_soft_done)*(1-per_step)
    return torch.where((public_age>=10)&(closure_m>.002),torch.ones_like(combined),combined)

             for role in ('extension', 'tuck')}

                for role, q in poses.items()}
    floor_origin = {role: g['low'][..., 2].amin(-1).detach() for role, g in baseline.items()}
    calls = dict(original130=0, candidate133=0)

    def inputs(height=.01, vz=.6, vx=0., pose='extension', x=-1., force=0., history_free=None,
               qualified=False, crossed=False, ready=False, active=1., closure=0., age=300):
        h, rv, xv = scalar(height), scalar(vz), scalar(vx)
        p = torch.stack((scalar(x), torch.zeros_like(h), h-floor_origin[pose]), -1)
        v = torch.stack((xv, torch.zeros_like(rv), rv), -1)
        q = poses[pose]
        state = (p, rotation, v, zero, q, torch.zeros_like(q), torch.zeros(1, 54))
        g = fk(state)
        local = g['centre']-p[:, None, :]-hips[None]
        free = force <= 1. if history_free is None else history_free
        mode = direct.modes(g['low'], g['high'], torch.full((1, 2), float(force)), p[:, 0])
        m = dict(wheel_bottom2=g['low'][..., 2], wheel_high2=g['high'], world_velocity3=v,
                 wheel_bottom_velocity2=wheel_bottom_velocity2(g, fk),

                 world_angular_velocity3=zero, request_active=f(active), heading_cos=f(1.),
                 upright_cos=f(1.), root_y=f(0.), body_yaw_velocity=f(0.), process_ready=b(ready),

        # Same actual wheel footprint domain as the frozen130 adapter. At x=-1
        # the actual complete own pose is safely behind the platform.
        kw = dict(launched=b(qualified), crossed=b(crossed), closure_m=f(closure),
                  public_age=torch.tensor([age]))

    def pair(m, mode, kw, effort=1., legacy=0.):
        old = original.evaluate(m, mode, f(legacy), f(effort), **kw)
        calls['original130'] += 1

                assert torch.equal(old['selected_terms'][key], new['selected_terms'][key]), key
        for key in old:
            if key in ('total', 'selected_terms'):
                continue
            if torch.is_tensor(old[key]):
                assert torch.equal(old[key], new[key]), key
            else:
                assert old[key] == new[key], key
        assert torch.equal(sum(new['selected_terms'].values()), new['total'])
        raw_delta = (.01*effort*new['conservative_upward_velocity'].mul(6).clamp_max(2.5)
                     *new['dense_air_geometry_history_surrogate']*new['true_wheel_clearance_fraction']
                     *(1-mode['top_support'])*m['task_lane_and_far_bound']*m['request_active'])
        expected_delta = raw_delta*(1-.5*new['closure_quality_cost'])
        assert torch.allclose(new['total']-old['total'], expected_delta, atol=2e-7, rtol=1e-5)
        assert bool(torch.isfinite(new['total']).all())
        return old, new

    def run(**kwargs):
        effort = kwargs.pop('effort', 1.)
        legacy = kwargs.pop('legacy', 0.)
        m, mode, kw, g = inputs(**kwargs)
        old, new = pair(m, mode, kw, effort, legacy)
        return old, new, m, mode, kw, g

    # AST-load only the original pure formula; no LeggedRobot initialization.

    assert torch.allclose(original_formula, f(.6))

    assert analytical(.064)-.025/.27 < 0  # derivative increases with h, so all earlier h too

        go = float(torch.autograd.grad(old['total'].sum(), h, retain_graph=True)[0])

        assert abs(go-(analytical(mm/1000.)-.025/.27)) < 3e-5

    air_names = ('fudan_line_z_real_wheel_ascent', 'fudan_flight_real_clearance',
                 'fudan_base_height_flight_own_wheel270', 'fudan_leg_tuck_own_geometry')
    for h0 in (-.01, 0.):

        assert torch.equal(old['total'], new['total'])
        # Rigid float32 translation can leave nanometres of floor roundoff;
        # this numerical allowance is not a new physical health threshold.
        assert all(new['selected_terms'][key].abs() <= 1e-8 for key in air_names)
    # Body-up, unloading and wheel spin are not true two-wheel ascent. The

    m, mode, kw, _ = inputs()
    m['wheel_bottom_velocity2'] = torch.tensor([[.6, -.1]])
    assert pair(m, mode, kw)[1]['selected_terms']['fudan_line_z_real_wheel_ascent'] == 0

    for active, region, legacy in ((0., True, -.12), (0., True, .03), (1., False, 0.)):
        m, mode, kw, _ = inputs(active=active)

    assert torch.equal(run(ready=False)[1]['total'], run(ready=True)[1]['total'])
    checks.append('apex_zero_vx_floor_body_only_spin_nonwheel_support_request_domain_and_ready0_semantics_preserved')

    # Actual own wheel footprint moves into top support; velocities supplied
    # here are structural descending states, not a simulated contact trajectory.
    min_rear_offset = float(baseline['extension']['low'][..., 0].amin())
    landing = []
    for rear_mm in range(-5, 26):
        x = .35+rear_mm/1000.-min_rear_offset

                                     qualified=True, crossed=True)
        assert torch.equal(old['total'], new['total'])
        assert new['selected_terms']['fudan_line_z_real_wheel_ascent'] == 0
        assert new['dense_stage_with_qualified_landing_bridge'] == 1
        landing.append(dict(rear_margin_mm=rear_mm, P=float(mode['top_support']), reward=float(new['total'])))
    assert all(y['reward'] >= x['reward']-1e-6 for x, y in zip(landing, landing[1:]))
    assert landing[-1]['P'] > .9 and landing[-1]['reward'] > landing[0]['reward']
    for quiet_v in (0., .01, .03):
        old, new, *_ = run(height=.27, vz=0., vx=quiet_v, x=.6, force=100., qualified=True, crossed=True)
        assert torch.equal(old['total'], new['total'])
        assert new['selected_terms']['quiet_top_after_qualified_cross'] > 0
    _, climb, *_ = run(height=.27, vz=0., vx=0., x=.6, force=100., qualified=False, crossed=False)
    assert climb['selected_terms']['supported_top_after_qualified_cross'] == 0
    assert climb['selected_terms']['quiet_top_after_qualified_cross'] == 0
    # Disclose rather than hide the stronger upward loaded-history discontinuity.
    old_free, new_free, *_ = run(height=.1, vz=.6, history_free=True)
    old_loaded, new_loaded, *_ = run(height=.1, vz=.6, history_free=False)
    contact_switch = dict(scope='structural history switch, not a demonstrated feasible physical contact',
        height_m=.1, upward_m_s=.6,
        old_total_drop=float(old_free['total']-old_loaded['total']),
        new_total_drop=float(new_free['total']-new_loaded['total']))
    assert contact_switch['new_total_drop'] > contact_switch['old_total_drop'] > 0
    checks.append('descending_qualified_landing_bridge_and_quiet_unchanged_standing_unqualified_rejected_upward_switch_risk_disclosed')

    # Deterministic domain/bound and finite-gradient checks; no randomized cases.
    extrema = [float('inf'), -float('inf')]
    for h0 in (-.01, 0., .001, .005, .01, .064, .1, .26, .27, .3, .6):
        for vz0 in (-2.4, 0., .1, .6, 4.):
            for force in (0., 100.):
                _, new, *_ = run(height=h0, vz=vz0, force=force, closure=.001, effort=.8)
                r = float(new['total'])
                assert -1e-7 <= r <= .19+1e-7
                extrema = [min(extrema[0], r), max(extrema[1], r)]
    for h0, vx0, vz0 in ((.002, -.5, .1), (.01, 0., .3), (.03, .5, .6), (.15, 0., -.3)):
        h, vx, vz = (f(x).requires_grad_() for x in (h0, vx0, vz0))
        _, new, *_ = run(height=h, vx=vx, vz=vz)
        gradients = torch.autograd.grad(new['total'].sum(), (h, vx, vz))
        assert all(bool(torch.isfinite(g).all()) for g in gradients)
    # Same supported-prefix input is still identical for 40 controls, including
    # any imagined jump after that prefix. No future-dependent reward is added.
    prefix_old = prefix_new = 0.
    for i in range(40):
        old, new, *_ = run(height=0., vz=.3*max(0., math.sin(i*math.pi/10)), force=100.)
        assert torch.equal(old['total'], new['total'])
        prefix_old += .99**i*float(old['total'])
        prefix_new += .99**i*float(new['total'])
    assert prefix_old == prefix_new
    risk_old = original.combine_closure_risk(f(.02), f(.0015), torch.tensor([300]))
    risk_new = candidate.combine_closure_risk(f(.02), f(.0015), torch.tensor([300]))
    assert torch.equal(risk_old, risk_new)
    assert torch.equal(torch.get_rng_state(), rng_before)
    assert calls['original130'] == calls['candidate133']
    checks.append('finite_total_gradients_request_bounds_nonrequest_risk_clock_API_RNG_no_hidden_future_prefix_change')
    return dict(schema='mod4.reward133.one_factor_total_gradient_cpu_fixture.v1', status='PASS', checks=checks,
        source_only_fixture_not_skill=True, actual_own_static_length_binding=own_proof,
        total_gradient_grid=grid, old_new_10_to_20mm=dict(old10=float(old10['total']), old20=float(old20['total']),
            new10=float(new10['total']), new20=float(new20['total'])),
        derivative_scope='own FK rigid translations with supplied velocities/history, not force/action/dynamics proof',
        apex_rewards=apex, descending_landing=landing, upward_history_switch_known_risk=contact_switch,
        deterministic_checked_reward_extrema=extrema, shared_40_control_prefix_discounted_return=prefix_new,
        invocation_counts=calls, RNG_unchanged=True, source130_sha256=sha(folder/'original'/name),
        candidate133_sha256=sha(folder/'source'/name),
        unresolved=['physical reachability and policy action-to-ascent gradient', 'WM/critic and H32 credit',
                    'all-policy bounce/shuttle optimality', 'positive-ascent loaded-history switch',
                    'inherited signed-penalty adaptation and tracker flat region'])

===== FULL UTF8 C:\Users\lulu\Desktop\claude\mjcf\MOD4_H1_SCUT_STEP270_ON_210_E3_CONTINUATION_20261021\MOD4_H1_OVERNIGHT_FUDAN_SUPERMIMIC_REPAIR_GOAL_20261027\reward_only131_closed_Fudan130_TEXT_REVIEW_SOURCE_ONLY_20261030_V1\REVIEW.md SEGMENT 0 =====
# Closed131 / frozen130 reward-only source and saved-text review

Status: SOURCE_REVIEW_ONLY. No project code, fixture, SDK, physics, model, GPU, remote, or Git operation was executed by this reviewer. Frozen130 and all earlier evidence are unchanged. Root owns execution and Git archival; these new files are pending that archival.

**There is a definite local reward-design defect:** source130 multiplies the real-wheel ascent reward by remaining height `(1-clearance)`. At an own MOD4 extension pose, with genuine positive rigid wheel ascent, the TOTAL selected reward decreases as both wheel bottoms rise through approximately 5–64 mm. The original Fudan `line_z` formula does not contain this factor. This warrants a one-factor source-only correction. It does not prove the trained131 policy visited this particular state family, or that reward is the only cause of no platform success.

## Actual evidence and its scope

The first source was root's `CURRENT_CLOSED131_TWO_QUALIFIED_FUDAN130_FORMALS_NO_PLATFORM_SUCCESS_ACTUAL.json`. Both new131 formals passed root's initial fullstate CPU checks. h1 / seed28 completed 2139 / 2101 controls and 8300 / 8150 new WM updates, 766 / 751 new policy updates. Original cross, landing and recovery counts are zero for both. The contact-release takeoff counts 952 / 935 do not measure useful jump height.

The compact actual report `CLOSED131_FUDAN130_SELECTED_TERMS_FULL_CURVES_REQUEST_SCOPE_TEXT_ONLY_ACTUAL.json` was checked against selected saved UTF-8 curve rows. It selects **population mean command_forward_velocity > .45**, with 720 saved rows per role from completed controls201–1999. Each component is the last completed control's 4096-environment population mean; selection does not make these per-environment request-conditioned means. Curves end at controls2136 / 2099, before formals2139 / 2101. This is neither whole-window return nor complete per-control coverage. Final formal requestOFF rewards cannot diagnose the request reward.

| Selected mean per saved request-dominant row | h1 | seed28 |
|---|---:|---:|
| Total | .02147365868 | .02181477275 |
| Forward tracker | .01212084069 | .01236643663 |
| Enhanced tracker | -.00023840475 | -.00021828936 |
| Yaw tracker | .00876977553 | .00883257325 |
| Root-up encouragement | .00016141990 | .00015555875 |
| Own extension | .00076023748 | .00080935677 |
| Real-wheel ascent | .00007034731 | .00003644124 |
| Wheel-height reward | .00002262010 | .00002406467 |
| Own tuck | .00002143892 | .00002409232 |
| Qualified top / quiet | 0 / 0 | 0 / 0 |
| Closure deduction | -.00022041007 | -.00021984790 |

Real-wheel ascent is nonzero in **707 / 704 of 720** selected population rows. Therefore the claim that source130 never activates a jump signal is false. Small population means cannot reveal individual peak height, contact timing, the distribution of gate products, action gradients, or whether the counterexample below occurred. Forward/yaw contributions dominate the recorded mean, but this alone does not prove an alternative policy's return or motivate a weight sweep.

## Definite defect: higher ascending wheels can receive less TOTAL reward

`source/jump_process_command_reward122_v1.py:55`:

```python
line_z=(6*conservative_up).clamp_max(2.5)*air*(1-clearance)
```

Use own extension lengths `[.3385148644,.3385327160]` and own tuck lengths `[.2623806596,.2502311766]`, returned by root's executed own-FK binding. Their tuck kernel at the extension pose is `tau=exp(-4*sum(abs(extension-tuck)))`, approximately .51802. These are MOD4 static kinematic witnesses, not imported leg dimensions or proven dynamical trajectories.

Consider a rigid vertical translation of that pose, safely behind the platform, with both wheels clear, nonwheel clearance >=5mm, unloaded wheel history, zero rotation, unchanged vx/heading, root and wheel centre/bottom vz=.6m/s, no top support, no qualified/cross memory, requestON, effort1 and closure0. The smaller wheel bottom is h. The small difference between the two actual wheel bottoms does not change which one limits both clearance and height below270mm. For 5mm<h<270mm, `air=stage=1`. The capped line reward is .025 per control before multiplying `(1-h/.27)`.

Let `a=exp(-1.62)` and `H(h)=(exp(-6*(.27-h))-a)/(1-a)`. All h-dependent selected terms sum to

`R_h=.025*(1-h/.27)+.025*H(h)+(.0015+.017*tau)*h/.27`.

All remaining terms have zero height derivative in this state family. Thus

`dR/dh=-.025/.27 + .15*exp(-6*(.27-h))/(1-exp(-1.62)) + (.0015+.017*tau)/.27`.

At h=.01m this is approximately **-.01512 reward/m**. It is negative throughout the open interval above5mm through64mm and crosses zero near64.3mm. At exactly5mm use the right derivative; below5mm the air ramp changes the formula. A finite increase from10mm to20mm at fixed positive ascent therefore decreases TOTAL reward. This is a local low-clearance reward ridge, not merely a declining diagnostic subterm. Constant effort or closure attenuation scales the sign without fixing it.

The earlier source130 fixture checked height monotonicity at **apex vz=0**, where line_z is zero, and checked gradients for finiteness rather than this sign. Those passes remain valid within their narrower assertions; they did not detect this defect.

## Original Fudan comparison and narrow repair

Pinned upstream commit `8204e853dfd2ed06d85a322e1a998c3d20a3be2c`, original `legged_robot.py:1759–1762`: line_z is positive root vertical velocity times `was_in_flight`. Its config gives line_z scale6, and the weighted jump term is capped2.5. It has **no height-shortfall multiplier**. Upstream byte SHA and root's independently fetched exact-match receipt are indexed separately; no network request was made here.

The proposed133 changes only the assignment to:

```python
line_z=(6*conservative_up).clamp_max(2.5)*air
```

It keeps130's conservative minimum of actual root, both wheel-bottom and both wheel-centre vertical velocities, current own geometry/unloaded-history air surrogate, own leg targets, weights, caps, lane domain, effort, closure, top bridge, original126 single-flight/ready/cross codec, and original physical success. It adds no readiness or forward-speed prerequisite. The derivative above loses only `-.025/.27`, making it positive on the stated own-pose height interval. At floor, apex or nonpositive conservative ascent, the removed factor had no effect because line_z was already zero.

This is a partial return toward the pinned Fudan formula, not a claim of literal upstream reward equivalence. Source130 deliberately adapted signed penalties and observable flight semantics; those are unchanged in133.

## Conditional risks retained, not silently repaired

* Core lines33–34 bound the enhanced tracking penalty by the matching positive tracker. Their sum is `2*[exp(-4e)+exp(-.4e)-1]_+` before dt/alignment. For sufficiently large velocity error (e about .451 or more) the pair is flat zero. For vx=-.5, desired=.5, vy0, it has zero corrective velocity gradient, whereas upstream signed tracking+enhance retains one. This is a concrete source difference; population means do not prove it caused131's failure. It is outside the one-factor133 change.
* `mod4_fudan_contact_phase_reward_candidate_v2.py:28–31` applies original selected JUMP penalties once through bounded effort attenuation, not the upstream literal signed sum (`only_positive_rewards=False`). Derivatives of unclipped penalty contributions are scaled by `5*R` at dt=.01. Balance/effort tradeoffs therefore differ from upstream. No evidence here justifies changing them simultaneously.
* Dense130 incentives do not require ready, while strict126 qualification does. Saved request rows show roughly .23 prepared-memory population in sampled controls. This is not proof of physical readiness distribution, nor a reason to forge readiness or loosen success. No physical event/reset is changed.
* A true descending landing has conservative_up=0; removing the factor leaves its reward and qualified landing bridge exactly unchanged. A structural input with **positive ascent and newly loaded wheel history before P rises** can lose the line reward on the discrete air-history switch.133 can enlarge that discontinuity. The new fixture must report it explicitly; do not claim continuity for every contact/velocity combination. This is distinct from the legal descending landing fixture.
* The correction does not remove every stationary/bounce/shuttle alternative, solve delayed credit beyond H32/.99, fix a world model, or prove feasible control/actuator acceleration. Existing source130 root-up/extension signals remain. A shared observable40-control prefix cannot be rewarded differently according to an unobserved future jump. Critic, WM and physical reachability remain unknown here.

## Bound and task invariants

Line_z still has cap2.5 before dt. For requestON, tracking+enhance is in[0,.02], yaw in[0,.01]. Before effort, the sum of bounded jump terms is at most `.0036+.005+.025+.0015+.025+.017=.0771`, multiplied by `(1-P)*region`; qualified top+quiet is at most `.16*P`. Hence subtotal <=`.03+.0771*(1-P)+.16*P<=.19`. Effort<=1 and closure deducts at most half the nonnegative subtotal, so request total remains in[0,.19]. This loose bound intentionally allows mutually incompatible term maxima and therefore remains conservative. Original nonrequest recovery may be signed and is unchanged.

Original physical two-wheel crossing/full footprint/top contact12/stable200callbacks,2mm single-environment reset,100Hz/20s/request clock,17bit process metadata/six cues and model/optimizer state are untouched. Candidate source/test cannot certify actual jumping. Root must execute the new total-gradient fixture and normal admission; subsequent actual control and physical event evidence are required for any skill claim.
